# Atelier Master Goal

- P3-07 / blocking gate recovery variants WIP 本轮开始把 blocking gate recovery product-window gate 从默认 `rerun_failed_node` 参数化到 `accept_risk/continue/cancel` variants，保持 official applet 只提交 human choice、Station 继续拥有 recovery semantics。已改动：`apps/station/app/subserver/official_applets/atelier_gate_server/main.go` 的受控 blocking gate decision options 补齐正式 Station payload 中已有的 `continue` option；`tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-artifact-gate-product-window-gate.mjs` 新增 `--gate-recovery-action=` 参数，默认仍为 `rerun_failed_node`，为 `accept_risk/continue/cancel` 生成独立 evidence 文件名、choice、expected `gate_recovery_action`、task/node/gate-plan status；`package.json` 增加单 variant product-window gate scripts 与 aggregate variants script。验证已过：`node --check tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-artifact-gate-product-window-gate.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/applet-desktop-product-window-gate.mjs` PASS；`go test ./subserver/official_applets/...` PASS；`go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'TestResolveCollaborationInterruptTxAcceptsGateBlockedTask|TestResolveCollaborationInterruptTxRerunsGateBlockedNode|TestResolveCollaborationInterruptTxCancelsGateBlockedTask|TestResolveCollaborationInterruptTxExpiresStaleLeaseBeforeGateRecovery'` PASS。未完成：`pnpm run applet:atelier-artifact-gate-recovery-accept-product-window-gate` 进入 normal Desktop product shell 后失败，`applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-artifact-gate-recovery-accept-risk-product-window-gate.json` 为 `ok=false`，当前 evidence 显示 `product-shell-evidence.json` 最终写出 `ok=true` 但 underlying Desktop gate 仍按 wait timeout 失败，且 official subscription diagnostic 仍为 `controller.effect-no-snapshot`；因此 `accept_risk/continue/cancel` variants 尚无有效 product-window proof，不能从 notCovered 中移除。下一步需要定位通用 product-window evidence wait 与 Atelier snapshot invalid 的组合问题，再重跑 variants。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

- P3-07 / product-window recovery gate stabilization 本轮修复 P3-07 后续复核暴露的两个 gate-level 问题，并同步 P3-08 completion audit：一是 `tooling/scripts/applet-desktop-product-window-gate.mjs` 的 Desktop build 子进程默认使用 macOS `/var/folders/.../T`，在本机出现 esbuild temp file `operation not permitted`，导致 gate JSON 被覆盖成 before-launch failure；已在 harness 内部增加 worktree-local stable temp fallback（`TMPDIR/TEMP/TMP = .local/applet-product-window-gate/tmp`，外部显式 env 仍优先），并传给 `pnpm build`、`tauri build` 与 product app runtime 子进程。二是 `tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-artifact-gate-product-window-gate.mjs` 的 recovery replay probe 旧断言要求 follow-up subscribe `afterEventSeq === decisionEvidence.eventSeq`；当前真实 product path 在 resolved projection 后会把 cursor 推进到 `afterEventSeq=6` 且 replay 空集，而 rendered decision evidence cursor 为 `eventSeq=6`，因此 gate 改为要求 cursor `>= decisionEvidence.eventSeq` 且 `expectNoReplay=true`，证明不会重放已消费 durable event，而不是绑定旧精确值。验证：`PEERS_APPLET_PRODUCT_WINDOW_E2E_CLOSE_AFTER_RENDER_DELAY_MS=30000 PEERS_APPLET_PRODUCT_WINDOW_E2E_TIMEOUT_MS=150000 PEERS_APPLET_PRODUCT_WINDOW_E2E_REQUIRED_URLS_TIMEOUT_MS=60000 PEERS_APPLET_PRODUCT_WINDOW_E2E_POST_REQUIRED_URLS_WAIT_MS=12000 pnpm run applet:atelier-artifact-gate-recovery-product-window-gate` PASS；`applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-artifact-gate-recovery-product-window-gate.json` PASS（`ok=true`, REAL_PRODUCT_PATH, `atelier.decision.resolved.rendered`, `eventSeq=6`）；`node --check tooling/scripts/applet-desktop-product-window-gate.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-artifact-gate-product-window-gate.mjs` PASS；`node tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` PASS；`node tooling/scripts/atelier-official-frontend-gate.mjs` PASS；`node tooling/scripts/atelier-bridge-runtime-gate.mjs` PASS；targeted `git diff --check` PASS。`applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-completion-audit-2026-07-06.md` 已从旧的 P3-06/P3-07 stale 状态更新为 focused product-window slices PARTIAL，并保留真实 executor/provider loop、accept/continue/cancel recovery variants、rich Host artifact sandbox、cross-restart cursor recovery、complete Host+Station+applet E2E 未证明。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

- P2-05 / projection subscription lifecycle static recovery 本轮修复 P3-07 后遗留的 static/controlled gate drift：`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 发现 browser prototype `appletBridge.ts` 没有按 lifecycle-aware wrapper 顺序先挂 local Host event handler；`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 发现 official `subscribeAtelierProjectionEvents` 先 `sdk.events.subscribe` 后 `sdk.events.on`，与 contract 要求的 local handler first 不一致。修复：official `apps/applets/atelier/frontend/src/infrastructure/capability/atelierClient.ts` 改为先 `sdk.events.on(ATELIER_PROJECTION_EVENT_TOPIC, ...)`，再 `await sdk.events.subscribe(...)`，再执行 Station `ATELIER_PROJECTION_SUBSCRIPTION_METHOD` stream subscribe；失败路径先移除 local handler，再 Host unsubscribe，避免 rejection 后继续投递。browser prototype `packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/src/appletBridge.ts` 同步先 `host.onEvent(projectionEventTopic, ...)` 并用 `closed` guard 包住 listener，再调用 Host `events.subscribe` 与 Station stream subscribe；rejected subscribe 会关闭 local handler并发出 `atelier.projection.subscription-rejected`。验证：`node tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` PASS；`node tooling/scripts/atelier-official-frontend-gate.mjs` PASS；`node tooling/scripts/atelier-bridge-runtime-gate.mjs` PASS；`pnpm --filter @peers-touch/atelier-official-applet run check` PASS；targeted `git diff --check -- apps/applets/atelier/frontend/src/infrastructure/capability/atelierClient.ts packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier/src/appletBridge.ts tmp/atelier-master-goal.md` PASS。复核限制：尝试用 full product-window recovery gate 复核真实 Desktop window path，但 `pnpm run applet:atelier-artifact-gate-recovery-product-window-gate` 在 Desktop build/esbuild 阶段失败，错误为 `[vite:esbuild-transpile] open /var/folders/.../T/esbuild-...: operation not permitted`，gate JSON 被覆盖为 `ok=false` / `failed before Desktop product-window launch`；用 worktree-local `TMPDIR` 重跑 direct wrapper 未得到新的 product-window evidence，并已停止残留 controlled fixture 进程。因此本轮只声明 static/controlled subscription lifecycle recovery，不把 product-window P3-07 复核作为当前有效证据，不提升全局 readiness。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

## Latest Evidence

- P2-05 / official Context projection group taxonomy contract-first cleanup 本轮把 official `ContextPanel` 的 `files/other` 本地分组过滤从 `files.filter((file) => file.group === 'files'/'other')` 收敛为 generated `ATELIER_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUPS` + `ATELIER_DEFAULT_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUP`：新增 `ContextFileGroup` generated type alias、`CONTEXT_FILE_GROUP_LABEL_KEYS` / `CONTEXT_FILE_GROUP_MORE_KEYS` / `CONTEXT_FILE_GROUP_DISPLAY_LIMITS` exhaustive maps，`OFFICIAL_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUPS` 以 generated default group 为首，其余按 generated taxonomy 顺序渲染；当前行为仍展示 Files/Other 两组并保留 read-only Station projection disclosure，但未来 contract 增组会触发 TS/gate 显式补齐，而不是被 UI 静默丢弃。`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier-projection-contract-gate` 已改为要求 generated taxonomy/default consumption，并拒绝旧 local `files/other` filter、`ATELIER_CONTEXT_FILE_GROUPS[0]` 顺序默认或本地 union 回流。验证：`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-official-frontend-gate.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm --filter @peers-touch/atelier-official-applet run check` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:prototype-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 official applet build 与 desktop-injection gate；integrity expected=actual=`sha256:208cefd4ae1e888f1b49cd52a56e6fb0913ea9daa678dac1c7ddf9ccd94acc8a`）；targeted old-filter scan 证明旧 hardcoded group filter 只剩 gate negative assertions。注意：这是 official Context projection grouping 的 contract drift guard，不实现 Workspace file discovery、Run input_snapshot write、真实 Host+Station+applet E2E 或 Context runtime mutation。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

- P2-05 / official artifact preview open default mode contract-first cleanup 本轮把 official controller `openArtifactPreview()` 的 mode fallback 从本地 `previewTarget?.mode?.trim() || 'sandbox_manifest'` 收敛为 generated `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_PREVIEW_OPEN_DEFAULT_MODE`：`atelier-projection.contract.json` 在 `methodPayloads.atelier.artifact.preview.open` 新增 explicit `defaultMode=sandbox_manifest`，`generate-atelier-projection-contract.mjs` 校验 `defaultMode` 必须属于 `allowedModes` 并生成 official/prototype `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_PREVIEW_OPEN_DEFAULT_MODE`，`useAtelierController.ts` 使用 generated default；`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 与 `atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 contract/generator/generated/controller anchors，并拒绝 literal fallback 或从 `ATELIER_ARTIFACT_PREVIEW_OPEN_MODES[0]` 顺序推导 default 回流。验证：`node --check tooling/scripts/generate-atelier-projection-contract.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-official-frontend-gate.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-codegen` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-codegen-check` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm --filter @peers-touch/atelier-official-applet run check` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:prototype-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 official applet build 与 desktop-injection gate；integrity expected=actual=`sha256:c65f3c74753767cfeb393d80d4c3ace6cb2ddd9c20939f939674d69bee4d1a87`）；targeted scan 证明旧 controller fallback literal 已无 source 命中；targeted `git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 artifact preview open default-mode 的 contract drift guard，不实现 Host sandbox rich renderer、不改变 Desktop Host renderer ownership，也不证明完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

- P2-05 / prototype runtime createFromGoal intent preset mapping contract-first cleanup 本轮把 browser prototype `runtime.ts` 的 `intentPresetMetadata()` 从本地 switch/literal mapping 收敛为 generated `ATELIER_CREATE_FROM_GOAL_INTENT_PRESET_MAPPING`：`work/code/design` 的 `providerStrategyPreset` 与 `gatePlanPreset` 不再在 prototype runtime 复制，缺省 fallback 也通过 generated `ATELIER_DEFAULT_TASK_INTENT_PRESET` 对应 mapping 解析；`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 与 `atelier-bridge-runtime-gate` 已改为要求 runtime 消费 generated mapping，并拒绝 runtime 内 `coding_provider_preferred`、`lint_typecheck_build`、`design_review_preferred`、`prototype_visual_review`、`station_generalist_research`、`plan_review_evidence` 等 contract-owned literal 回流。验证：`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-bridge-runtime-gate.mjs` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:prototype-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 official applet build 与 desktop-injection gate；integrity expected=actual=`sha256:8b408e1073a1de926a92ac7abb2cecaf5899b97f519403b166af6698350d6cf1`）；targeted literal scan 证明旧 preset literals 只剩 gate expected mapping 表；targeted `git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 prototype runtime declarative intent metadata 的 contract drift guard，不实现真实 provider selection / execution、不改变 Station orchestration truth、不证明完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

- P2-05 / browser prototype projection stream taskId source priority parity cleanup 本轮把 browser prototype `appletBridge.ts` 的 projection stream `taskId` fallback 从本地 `snapshot?.selectedTaskId || snapshot?.workspace.tasks[0]?.id` 收敛为 generated `ATELIER_PROJECTION_CONTRACT.eventSubscription.taskIdSourcePriority`：显式 `projectionStream.taskId` 仍优先，缺省时按 contract resolver 读取 `snapshotSelectedTaskId` / `snapshotFirstTaskId`，其余 official-only source 返回 undefined，不改变 prototype 行为；`atelier-bridge-runtime-gate` 与 `atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 `projectionTaskIdResolvers` / generated source priority anchor，并拒绝旧 selected/first task 本地表达式回流。验证：`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-bridge-runtime-gate.mjs` PASS；`node --check tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:prototype-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 official applet build 与 desktop-injection gate；integrity expected=actual=`sha256:1c2e266b8778e36dc461e39021e87ba0776a6ac781cfd48b52ae7853ace5fc90`）；targeted scan 证明旧 `projectionTaskIdFromSnapshot` / local selected-first fallback 已无 source 命中；targeted `git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 browser prototype bridge 与 official eventSubscription contract 的 parity hardening，不实现真实 Station SSE 任意网络故障、cross-restart cursor recovery、真实 Desktop product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 `NOT_READY`。

- P3-05 / PAUSED live resume with real executor/provider product-window slice 本轮完成：Station `TurnService` 新增 Station-owned internal tool `station_human_decision_resume`，provider loop 可在同一 turn 内等待 `LiveResumeBroker` 的 human decision，并在 resolve 后调用 `markCollaborationResumeContextConsumed` 标记 durable interrupt consumed；controlled Atelier gate server 新增 `live_resume_provider` 场景，seed `PAUSED` task + running executor node + pending interrupt，并通过 OpenAI-compatible streaming provider endpoint 驱动真实 `TurnService.ExecuteTurn` 首次返回 tool call、official applet 通过 `/v1/escalations:resolve` 提交 choice、Station `ResolveCollaborationInterrupt` 唤醒 live waiter、provider 第二次调用返回最终响应。证据 `applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-live-resume-product-window-gate.json` 为 `ok=true` / `REAL_PRODUCT_PATH`，`liveResumeProbe` 记录 `providerCalls=[waitTool, final]`、`turnStatus=completed`、`resolvedInterrupts=1`、`consumedInterrupts=1`、`finalResponse=Atelier live resume provider turn completed after Station human decision.`；Desktop evidence `atelier-live-resume-product-window-resolved-evidence.json` 记录 official applet 在 normal Desktop product shell 内完成 rendered decision resolve。验证：`node --check tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-live-resume-product-window-gate.mjs` PASS；`go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'Test(StationHumanDecisionResumeToolConsumesLiveDecision|ResolveCollaborationInterruptWithLiveResumeTxWakesWaitingTurn)'` PASS；`go test ./subserver/official_applets/atelier_gate_server` PASS；manual controlled server probe PASS；`node tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-live-resume-product-window-gate.mjs` PASS。注意：这是 P3-05 live-resume focused product-window slice，不证明 real executor/provider recovery completion beyond live human-decision resume、rich Host artifact sandbox visual renderer 或 complete Host+Station+applet E2E。

- P2-05 / official rejected projection event runtime guard 本轮修复 official controller 对被 reducer 拒绝事件的 stream 状态误报：`projectionReducer` 新增 `applyAtelierProjectionEventWithResult()`，返回 `applied / duplicate / stale / unknown-task` outcome；旧 `applyAtelierProjectionEvent()` 继续保留 state-only API。`useAtelierController` 改为消费 outcome：applied/duplicate 才恢复 `live`，stale seq 或 unknown-task scope rejected event 会保留最后有效 projection 并进入 `degraded` / `invalid-projection`，同时不清理 resolving/sending 状态，避免 rejected event 被误当成成功 patch。`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 新增 reducer outcome matrix，`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 controller outcome wiring、stale/unknown-task degrade guard、gate outcome assertions 与 prototype README evidence；`docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` 同步记录 official malformed/rejected event handling 边界。验证：`pnpm --filter @peers-touch/atelier-official-applet run check` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 official frontend local projection event runtime guard，不证明真实 Station SSE 任意网络故障、跨重启 cursor recovery、真实 Desktop product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

## 0. 文件边界

本文件是 Atelier 专属临时执行总账，只追踪 `/Users/bytedance/Documents/Projects/peers-touch/peers-touch-applet` 内的 Atelier applet 能力建设。

严禁混用：

- 不追踪 Big A 投研系统。
- 不读写 `/Users/bytedance/Documents/Projects/peers-touch/my-peers-applets/tmp/big-a-master-goal.md`。
- 不把其他 agent 的 goal、股票研究、投研系统阶段混入本文件。

正式架构真源仍在 `docs/architecture/atelier/`；本文件只做执行编排和进度追踪。

---

## 1. 总目标

把 Atelier 落到可真实验收的 Desktop applet / personal workbench / projection surface：

- Station / agent orchestration 负责执行、Provider、Gate、Artifact、Trace、Checkpoint、Resume、durable outbox 和持久化真源。
- Atelier applet 只消费 projection snapshot/event，只通过声明式 capability 回写用户意图。
- Artifact/Gate production method 不暴露给 applet。
- 所有阶段按“先静态可验证，后真实联调”推进。
- 真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E 永远放在最后一组 gate。

| `docs/architecture/atelier/execution-plans/multi-engine-feasibility.md` | 多引擎可落地性与真实代码复用 |
| `docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` | 当前实现进度、已落地能力、剩余项 |
| `apps/applets/atelier/contracts/atelier-projection.contract.json` | official applet projection contract |
| `tooling/scripts/atelier-projection-contract-gate.mjs` | projection / Station boundary contract gate |

---

## 3. 不变量

1. **Projection-only applet**：Atelier applet 不拥有 execution/artifact/gate/provider 真源。
2. **Station-owned side effects**：副作用只经 Station / Provider / Core 落账。
3. **Durable outbox first**：对 applet 可见的事件必须来自 `agent_task_events` 或其 materialized projection。
4. **Typed contracts over JSON escape hatch**：新能力必须优先走 proto/DTO/typed guard；legacy `meta` 只作为兼容 replay。
5. **Evidence before readiness**：不得用静态 smoke claim 真实 E2E ready。
6. **Prototype sync**：UI/行为变更必须同步 `docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` 和对应 prototype / official frontend，或标记 `UNSYNCED`。

---

## Latest evidence override — 2026-07-06 P2-04b/P3-06

- P2-04b/P3-06 / artifact preview-open Host-rendered sandbox surface product-window proof: the current evidence supersedes the earlier prepared-only preview-open record. `applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-artifact-preview-open-product-window-evidence.json` now proves `accepted=true`, `prepared=true`, `opened=true`, `rendererOwner=desktop_host`, `rendererMode=host_sandbox_manifest`, `rendererStatus=rendered`, and `rendererCapabilities` includes `host_visual_renderer_surface`, with canonical `atelier-sandbox://.../preview` and `artifact://.../body` refs. `applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-artifact-gate-product-window-gate.json` includes the same rendered `previewOpenEvidence`.
- Scope claim: official `peers.atelier` in the normal Desktop product window submits the Station-projected sandbox preview target through `atelier.artifact.preview.open`; Lynx Host consumes Desktop Gateway `openAtelierArtifactPreview`; Desktop Host returns an opaque Host-owned rendered sandbox surface descriptor. This does not prove web iframe/image/html/diff renderer, Console Logs runtime stream, attachment Host Storage runtime, real executor/provider recovery completion, or complete Host + Station + applet E2E. Global Atelier readiness remains `NOT_READY`.
- P2-04b / prototype rich preview UNSYNCED boundary ordering guard: `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` now asserts the browser prototype `PrototypeOnlyRichPreviewNotice` appears before each markdown/web/image/diff rich render branch, not merely somewhere in `preview.tsx`; `atelier:projection-contract-gate` fixes that bridge-gate assertion as a required anchor; `docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` records the ordering evidence. This preserves the prototype target-shape asset while preventing iframe/image/diff rich preview from being mistaken for official applet support. It does not prove Host sandbox rich renderer, Console Logs runtime stream, attachment Host Storage, or complete Host + Station + applet E2E.
- P3 evidence aggregation / real-product gate suite coverage guard: root `package.json` `atelier:real-product-gates` now includes workspace/replay, normal product-window, cross-restart cursor, decision, live-resume, artifact/gate, rerun recovery, and blocking-gate recovery variant suite (`accept_risk` / `continue` / `cancel`) members; `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now parses package scripts, fails if any member of that real-product evidence suite is dropped, and exact-checks the three recovery variant scripts plus their aggregate expansion so the suite cannot silently collapse to fewer recovery actions; `docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` documents the expanded coverage. This is gate orchestration coverage only; it does not run the full real-product suite in this turn and does not prove arbitrary network failure, real executor/provider recovery completion, Host rich renderer, Console Logs, attachment Host Storage, or complete Host + Station + applet E2E.
- P2-05 / explicit run target default generated taxonomy guard: `atelier-projection.contract.json` now separates `defaultRunKind=agents` from `allowedRunKinds=[agents,model]`; `atelier:projection-codegen` validates that `defaultRunKind` belongs to `allowedRunKinds` and exports `ATELIER_DEFAULT_RUN_TARGET_KIND`; official `atelierClient.ts`, official `useAtelierController.ts`, and browser prototype `Page.tsx` now default run kind from that explicit generated default instead of local literals, selected-model fallback, or `ATELIER_RUN_TARGET_KINDS[0]`; `atelier:official-frontend-gate`, `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate`, and `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now fix the explicit default, generator validation/export anchors, and reject deriving default semantics from allowedRunKinds ordering or selected model presence; `docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` records the same. This is a contract-drift guard for Station-owned DirectRun/agents intent selection only; it does not invoke providers, run models, orchestrate agents, execute CLI, or prove real provider/agents E2E.
- P2-04b / artifact runtime notCovered wording consistency guard: `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now requires both `docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` and `tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-artifact-gate-product-window-gate.mjs` notCovered evidence to preserve the three remaining artifact runtime gaps as distinct claims: `web iframe/image/html/diff renderer`, `Console Logs runtime stream`, and `attachment Host Storage runtime`. This prevents product-window evidence reports from collapsing Host rich renderer, logs stream, and attachment storage into an ambiguous rich-runtime gap; it does not implement any of those runtimes.
- P3-07/P3-08 / readiness report non-completion guard: `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now also reads `applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-acceptance-evidence-report-2026-07-06.md` and `applet-readiness-evidence/official-applet/atelier-completion-audit-2026-07-06.md`, requires the acceptance report to preserve the non-global-completion/non-readiness claim, requires the completion audit to keep `Verdict: NOT_READY`, and requires both reports to preserve the same three explicit P2-04b runtime gaps (`web iframe/image/html/diff renderer`, `Console Logs runtime stream`, `attachment Host Storage runtime`). This is report consistency evidence only; it does not run or replace the missing real E2E gates.
- P3-05/P3-07/P3-08 / live-resume evidence command-anchor guard: readiness reports now cite the canonical packaged `pnpm run applet:atelier-live-resume-product-window-gate` command instead of the direct `node tooling/scripts/applet-atelier-live-resume-product-window-gate.mjs` entrypoint; `atelier:projection-contract-gate` fixes this as a positive/negative report assertion so the evidence anchor keeps the required `pnpm applets:build` precondition. This is report-anchor consistency only; it does not rerun the live-resume product-window gate or close remaining real E2E gaps.
- P2-03 / status notice generated taxonomy guard: official `StatusNotice` now types its `status` prop as generated `AtelierStatusNoticeKind` instead of duplicating `'reconciling' | 'degraded'` in page code; `atelier:projection-contract-gate` fixes both the generated type anchor and the negative hardcoded-union guard. This strengthens loading/empty/disconnected/auth-denied/reconciling/degraded UI state drift prevention; it is static/controlled evidence, not a real network failure matrix.
- P3-05 / decision product-window notCovered dedupe guard: `applet-atelier-decision-product-window-gate.mjs` success evidence no longer duplicates `complete Host + Station + applet E2E` in `notCovered`, and `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now rejects that exact duplicate sequence. This keeps decision-slice evidence boundaries precise; it does not add new runtime coverage.
- GAP-15 / ActionProvider forbidden surface guard: `atelier.provider.capabilities.forbiddenActions` now carries ActionProvider-specific high-risk execution metadata (`action.execute`, `action.run`, `policy.override`, `rollback.execute`) alongside generic `invoke/execute/run`, generated official/prototype contract artifacts carry the same metadata, and `atelier:projection-contract-gate` also includes `atelier.action.execute`, `atelier.action.run`, `atelier.policy.override`, `action.execute`, `action.run`, `policy.override`, `rollback.execute` in the applet forbidden production method surface. This aligns the contract-first static guard with the data-model requirement that ActionProvider execution remains Station/Policy/Risk-owned with human confirmation and rollback/audit constraints; it does not implement ActionProvider runtime or E2E.
- GAP-04 / AgentRole authority metadata guard: `atelier-projection.contract.json` now declares `agentRoleAuthority` with the nine canonical roles and the minimal authority matrix (`goal_owner` terminal signoff, `risk` hard veto, `verifier` acceptance veto, `supervisor` progress control, `executor` judgment forbidden, `integrator` merge, `historian` memory record), plus `executionOwner=station` and `appletMayExecuteAuthority=false`. `generate-atelier-projection-contract.mjs` validates this matrix and exports official/prototype `ATELIER_AGENT_ROLE_AUTHORITY`, `ATELIER_AGENT_ROLES`, and `AtelierAgentRole`; `atelier:projection-contract-gate` fixes contract/generator/generated anchors. This advances GAP-04 from Station allowlist/service-static evidence toward contract-first authority metadata; it does not implement proto enum, EnginePolicy runtime, Consensus/Objection/signoff logic, real veto/acceptance E2E, or complete Host+Station+applet E2E.
- GAP-04 / Station AgentRole allowlist exact-set guard: `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now parses Station `atelierAgentRoles = map[string]struct{}{...}` and requires its keys to exactly match `agentRoleAuthority.roles` from the projection contract. This prevents Station service-static role allowlist drift from the generated applet/prototype authority metadata; it does not add proto enum, EnginePolicy runtime, signoff/veto execution, or E2E coverage.
- Evidence governance / ledger readiness boundary guard: `atelier:projection-contract-gate` now reads this dedicated ledger and requires it to preserve `全局 Atelier readiness 仍为 \`NOT_READY\`` while rejecting explicit global READY/final-completion claims. This prevents accumulated local PASS entries from drifting into a global readiness assertion; it does not add runtime coverage or close any remaining E2E gap.
- Negotiation projection contract hardening: `atelier-projection.contract.json` now declares `negotiationProjection` with canonical voice stances (`proposal`, `objection`, `counter`, `signoff`), required voice fields, optional Station trace ids (`sessionId`, `roundId`, `voiceId`, `objectionId`), no-evidence objection disposition `concern`, `consensusOwner=station`, `appletMayResolveConsensus=false`, and forbidden orchestration/provider/runtime actions. `generate-atelier-projection-contract.mjs` validates and emits this metadata; official projection parsing rejects non-contract stances, rejects empty `role/text/evidenceRef/sessionId/roundId/voiceId/objectionId` strings, and still accepts missing `evidenceRef` for concern downgrade; browser prototype `projection.ts` now applies the same generated stance taxonomy and non-empty voice-field guard at snapshot/event ingress. `atelier:projection-contract-gate`, `atelier:official-frontend-gate`, and `atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` now fix these anchors with malformed stance and empty voice-field fixtures. This is projection schema hardening only; it does not implement Consensus/Objection/signoff runtime, EnginePolicy runtime, agent orchestration, provider execution, or full Host+Station+applet E2E.

- `docs/architecture/atelier/data-model.md` 中完成谓词不再引用不存在实体。

### Active Lane B — Station GateRunner / CheckExecutor

目标：补齐真实 Gate runner/check executor，不再只有外部 submit gate_result。

任务：

- [x] 盘点现有 gate plan / node policy / acceptance verdict 数据结构。

- [x] 后续：正式 GatePlan proto/persistence。
- [x] 后续：把 runner 接入真实 task/node gate plan 调度点，而不是仅内部组件和测试。

- `go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service`
- `go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/...`
- `pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate`

### Active Lane C — Blocking Gate Recovery

目标：blocking gate 暂停后能由人工决策或修复信号安全恢复/复跑。

任务：

- [x] 定义 `gate_blocked*` meta 与 interrupt/resume 的关系：只经 `atelier.escalation.resolve` / Station interrupt resolution 恢复，不新增 applet gate recovery method。

验收：

- `atelier:projection-contract-gate` 覆盖 forbidden applet recovery methods、Station recovery helpers/action strings、guarded resume、lease fence 和测试存在性。
- 真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet gate recovery E2E 放入 P3-06，当前未验。

每轮至少按改动范围选择：

```bash
go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service
go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/...
pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate
pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate
pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate
pnpm run applet:atelier-desktop-injection-gate
pnpm applets:build
cargo check
git diff --check
```

真实 E2E 只在 P3 执行，不得提前宣称。

## 8. 编排规则

- 文档盘点、Station 盘点、Frontend/Desktop 盘点可并行 subagent。
- 代码实现按 ownership 串行合入：proto/model → Station service → Desktop transport → applet projection → gates/docs。
- 每个 subagent 必须继承本文件 §3 不变量。
- Reconcile 时以 `docs/architecture/atelier/README.md` 和 Station-owned truth 为准。
- 任何实现若要求 applet 生产 artifact/gate，直接判为架构违规。

- P2-07 Artifact `preview_hint` / safe body fetch 已推进到 service/static 闭环：`data-model.md` 将 `metadata_only`、`body_ref/body_hash/body_size/body_kind` 纳入 Artifact 契约；Station Atelier projection 输出 `previewHint/bodyRef/bodyHash/bodySize/bodyKind` 且继续 redaction `markdown/content/body/html/diff/patch`；Desktop Host `station_event_to_atelier_projection_event` 不再转发 `markdown`，只把 body metadata 从 snake_case 映射为 camelCase；official frontend type/guard 接收 preview/body metadata、拒绝所有正文泄漏，并在 ArtifactPreviewPanel 只读展示 metadata，不加载 iframe/image/external URL。新增 `atelier.artifact.body.fetch` / `artifact.body.fetch`：official applet 通过 Host SDK capability 显式请求 body，Desktop Gateway 转发到 Station `/sub-agent/agent/atelier/artifact/body/fetch`，Station 先做 owned task 校验，再按 `task_id + artifact_id + body_uri` 读取 `agent_task_artifact_blobs`，强制 canonical `artifact://<taskId>/<artifactId>/body`、active retention、未过期、`markdown/diff/text/json` allowlist、stored hash/expected hash 校验，并按 UTF-8 byte cap 返回 safe text。contract/official gates 已覆盖 method/action/route/service safety/UI/client/controller/tests。验证：`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -run 'TestFetchAtelierArtifactBody|TestBuildAtelierProjection' -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/...` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-codegen-check` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`cargo check --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml` PASS（仅既有 generated dead-code warnings）。注意：真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E、iframe/image/html/sandbox rich preview、Console Logs 真实流仍未落，browser prototype 继续标记 richer mock preview 为 `UNSYNCED`。

- P2-02 本轮补齐 bridge runtime 非 snapshot capability 的异步错误与成功路径状态闭环：`createBridgeAtelierRuntime.call()` 现在 `await bridge.call(...)` 后再返回，确保 provider discovery / feedback / workspace open / artifact body fetch 等非 snapshot method 的 rejected promise 会进入统一 `statusFromBridgeError`，更新 runtime snapshot status 后继续向调用方抛错；成功路径会恢复调用前 status，避免非 snapshot capability 成功后残留 `loading`。`atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` 新增 `testBridgeNonSnapshotErrorsUpdateStatus` 与 `testBridgeNonSnapshotSuccessRestoresStatus`，分别覆盖 `atelier.provider.capabilities` 异步 `FORBIDDEN` reject 后 runtime status 变为 `auth-denied`，以及 provider capabilities 成功后 status 仍为 `ready`。验证：`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm --dir packages/prototypes/desktop/applets/atelier build` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS。注意：这仍是 bridge runtime controlled evidence，不证明真实 Desktop Host + Station 网络错误 E2E。

- GAP-16 本轮从静态完成谓词推进到最小 Station read-only completion evaluator：`projectAtelierCompletion` 不再把 L0/L1、L2 与 residual risk 字段硬编码为占位值，而是从 durable gate result events、feedback events 与 task meta 派生 `noOpenBlockers`、`l0L1AcceptancePassed`、`l2HumanSignoffComplete`、`residualRisksLogged`、`memoryCandidatesGenerated`；缺少 gate/signoff/memory candidate 证据时保持 false，不把 applet 变成完成判定真源。同时修正 Project projection helper 中缺失 payload 字段经 `fmt.Sprint(nil)` 变成 `"<nil>"` 的污染问题，改用 `atelierStringValue` + fallback。`TestBuildAtelierProjectionSnapshotDerivesProjectCompletion` 覆盖 passed gate + owner signoff + memory candidate，以及 blocking failed gate 派生 blocker/defect。验证：`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -run 'TestBuildAtelierProjectionSnapshot(MapsCollaborationTask|DerivesProjectCompletion)' -count=1` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；旧硬编码/`fmt.Sprint(payload[...])` 污染扫描无命中；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍是 projection evaluator，不证明完整 contract acceptance predicate engine、Project acceptance 状态机或真实 E2E。

- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 DirectRun persistence record 推进到最小 Station-owned no-session lifecycle marker：`model/domain/agent/orchestration.proto` 新增 typed `TASK_SURFACE_DIRECT_RUN`，Station generated Go proto 已同步；`CreateCollaborationTask` 对 `TaskProviderPlan.source=atelier.direct_run.intent` 在同一 DB transaction 中创建 `DirectRun`、`TaskRun{Surface=TASK_SURFACE_DIRECT_RUN, ConversationID=""}`、`ExecutionStep{Status=PENDING, EligibleExecutors=EXECUTOR_KIND_STATION_HOSTED}`，并通过 `TaskEventWriter.appendTx` 写 durable `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_TASK_CREATED` marker event。`ExecutionStep` 保持 pending，避免虚假声明 provider execution started；DirectRun marker path 不调用普通 create `startTaskExecution`，避免把 no-session intent 误跑成 collaboration execution；official frontend selected model 分支已从 `kind=agents + run.model` 修正为 `run.kind=model / run.model`，且 model intent 不携带 `run.flowId`；Applet contract 仍禁止 `invoke/execute/run/shell/file`。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 已固定 proto enum、Station helper、transactional create、marker payload、normal execution skip 与测试存在性；`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 已固定 official DirectRun payload 分支；`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。验证：`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm --dir apps/applets/atelier/frontend build` PASS；`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）；`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/... -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍不是真实 Provider execution，不证明 CLI `CodingProvider` 适配、Budget/Policy/Trace/Artifact/Gate DirectRun 端到端路径或真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮追加 DirectRun runtime preflight 与 recovery split：`validateDirectRunRuntimePreflight` 在 marker 创建前 fail-closed 校验 provider/model/budget/policy/trace refs、typed DirectRun surface、空 ConversationID、pending step、空 TurnID 与 Station-hosted executor；marker payload 明确 `runtime_preflight=passed`、`provider_execution=not_started`。`recoverRunningTasks` 通过 `isDirectRunTaskForRecovery` 识别 `agent_direct_runs.source=atelier.direct_run.intent`，跳过普通 collaboration `startTaskExecution`，避免重启恢复把 DirectRun no-session marker 误跑成 collaboration execution。新增测试 `TestValidateDirectRunRuntimePreflightRejectsMissingTraceRef`、`TestValidateDirectRunRuntimePreflightRejectsStartedStep`、`TestIsDirectRunTaskForRecoverySplitsNormalCollaborationRecovery`，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 preflight/recovery split。`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm --dir apps/applets/atelier/frontend build` PASS；`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）；`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/... -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍不是真实 Provider execution，不证明 CLI `CodingProvider` 适配、Budget/Policy/Trace/Artifact/Gate DirectRun 端到端路径或真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 DirectRun preflight/recovery split 推进到 Station-owned model-provider execution service-level：`CreateCollaborationTask` 在 DirectRun marker 后调用专用 `startDirectRunExecution`，recovery 也把 DirectRun 路由到专用 no-session runtime；`executePendingDirectRun` 只消费 pending DirectRun marker，不创建 hidden `CollaborationSession`，通过内部 `CallDirectRunProvider` executor 调用 Station provider primitive；成功路径写 durable `TASK_EVENT_TYPE_ARTIFACT_CREATED`（safe text body 经 artifact blob store）、`STEP_COMPLETED`、`GATE_RESULT` 和 task completed status，失败路径写 failure artifact + `STEP_FAILED` + task failed status；CLI provider 会在 provider call 前识别为 Desktop-owned runtime，转为 `unsupported_cli_provider` + paused task + pending interrupt，不调用 provider executor。新增 focused tests `TestExecutePendingDirectRunSuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks`、`TestExecutePendingDirectRunProviderFailurePersistsFailureArtifact`、`TestExecutePendingDirectRunRejectsCLIProviderWithoutProviderCall`；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 DirectRun runtime/test snippets；`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'TestExecutePendingDirectRun(SuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks|ProviderFailurePersistsFailureArtifact|RejectsCLIProviderWithoutProviderCall)|TestDirectRunLifecycleMarkerPersistsDurableTaskCreatedEvent|TestValidateDirectRunRuntimePreflightRejectsMissingTraceRef|TestIsDirectRunTaskForRecoverySplitsNormalCollaborationRecovery' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：这仍不是 CLI `CodingProvider` adapter，不证明真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、完整 Budget/Policy runtime enforcement 或真实 provider/live stream 行为。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 DirectRun model-provider execution 推进到 Budget/Policy pre-provider-call guard service-level：`executePendingDirectRun` 在 provider lookup/call 前执行 `evaluateDirectRunRuntimePolicyPreflight`，复用 Station `taskTimeBudgetExceeded` 与 Station-owned `AtelierPolicy.HardDeny` / task meta `policy_hard_deny`；time budget exceeded 会进入 `budget_blocked`，policy hard deny 会进入 `policy_blocked`，二者都会把 `TaskRun` 暂停、`ExecutionStep` 标 failed，并通过 `TaskEventWriter.appendTx` 同事务写 pending `INTERRUPT_REQUESTED` 与 failed blocking `GATE_RESULT`，provider executor 调用次数保持 0。CLI provider 也复用 runtime interrupt，但仍不是 `CodingProvider` adapter。新增 focused test `TestExecutePendingDirectRunBudgetPolicyPreflightEscalatesBeforeProviderCall`，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 Budget/Policy preflight、runtime interrupt 与 blocking gate evidence snippets；`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'TestExecutePendingDirectRun(BudgetPolicyPreflightEscalatesBeforeProviderCall|SuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks|ProviderFailurePersistsFailureArtifact|RejectsCLIProviderWithoutProviderCall)' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：该切片当时仍不是 budget usage ledger；后续 token/money usage ledger 已补，真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、CLI `CodingProvider` adapter、provider pricing catalog / real billing reconciliation 与真实 provider/live stream 行为仍未证明。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 Budget/Policy pre-provider-call guard 推进到 DirectRun token usage ledger service-level：新增 Station-owned `persistence.TaskBudgetUsage` / `agent_task_budget_usages` / `016_task_budget_usages.sql`，并在 `AllModels` 注册；`finishDirectRunSuccess` 在 provider response 产出 artifact event 后，同事务调用 `createDirectRunBudgetUsageTx` 写入 input/output/total tokens、budget_ref、direct_run_id、provider/model 与 artifact event evidence；`evaluateDirectRunRuntimePolicyPreflight` 会通过 `directRunBudgetUsedTokens` 汇总 task 已用 tokens，若已达 `CollaborationTask.BudgetTokens` 则在 provider call 前进入 `budget_blocked`，写 pending interrupt + failed blocking gate，provider executor 调用次数为 0。`data-model.md` 新增 `BudgetUsage` 契约；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 model/migration/AutoMigrate/token preflight/success ledger tests；`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'TestExecutePendingDirectRun(SuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks|BudgetPolicyPreflightEscalatesBeforeProviderCall)' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：该切片当时仍不是 money pricing ledger；后续 explicit provider pricing money usage ledger 已补，真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、CLI `CodingProvider` adapter、provider pricing catalog / real billing reconciliation 与真实 provider/live stream 行为仍未证明。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 DirectRun token ledger 推进到 explicit provider pricing money ledger service-level：`executePendingDirectRun` 成功路径现在把已加载的 `AgentProvider` 传入 `finishDirectRunSuccess`，`createDirectRunBudgetUsageTx` 通过 `directRunProviderMoneyUsage` 读取 provider `Config.pricing.input_token_usd / output_token_usd`（兼容 camelCase / price aliases），将 provider response 的 input/output tokens 结算为 `TaskBudgetUsage.UsedMoney`；未配置显式价格时保持 0，不硬编码模型价格。`evaluateDirectRunRuntimePolicyPreflight` 新增 `directRunBudgetUsedMoney` 汇总，若已用 money 达到 `CollaborationTask.BudgetMoney`，在 provider call 前进入 `budget_blocked`，写 pending interrupt + failed blocking gate，provider executor 调用次数为 0。focused test 已在 success path seed explicit pricing config 并断言 `UsedMoney≈0.08`，同时新增 `money budget exceeded` preflight row；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 explicit pricing parser、money preflight 与测试 snippets；`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'TestExecutePendingDirectRun(SuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks|BudgetPolicyPreflightEscalatesBeforeProviderCall)' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：这仍不是 provider pricing catalog / real billing reconciliation，不证明真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、CLI `CodingProvider` adapter 或真实 provider/live stream 行为。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 explicit provider pricing money ledger 推进到 pricing snapshot reconciler service-level：`TaskBudgetUsage` 新增 `input_token_price / output_token_price / pricing_source` 字段，`createDirectRunBudgetUsageTx` 会通过 `directRunProviderPricing` 将 provider config pricing snapshot 与 token/money usage 同账持久化；新增 `BudgetUsageReconciler`，按 persisted pricing snapshot 计算 expected money、聚合 actual money，并报告 `BudgetUsageMismatch`。新增 tests `TestBudgetUsageReconcilerBalancesCatalogSnapshot` 与 `TestBudgetUsageReconcilerReportsPricingMismatch`，DirectRun success test 同步断言 pricing snapshot；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 pricing snapshot 字段、parser 与测试 snippets；`data-model.md`、`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'Test(BudgetUsageReconciler|ExecutePendingDirectRun(SuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks|BudgetPolicyPreflightEscalatesBeforeProviderCall))' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：这仍不是 formal provider pricing catalog / real billing reconciliation，不证明真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、CLI `CodingProvider` adapter 或真实 provider/live stream 行为。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 pricing snapshot reconciler 推进到 provider pricing catalog snapshot service-level：`Provider` data-model 新增 `ProviderPricingCatalog`，Station `directRunProviderPricing` 现在优先解析 provider config 中 versioned `pricing_catalog/pricingCatalog` 的 model-specific entry，并把 `source@version:model` 写入 `TaskBudgetUsage.PricingSource`；legacy `pricing.input_token_usd/output_token_usd` 仍作为 fallback。`BudgetUsageReconciler` 新增 `BudgetUsageMissingPricing` / `MissingPricings`，有 token usage 但无 pricing snapshot 不再被零价判为 balanced。新增 tests `TestDirectRunProviderPricingResolvesModelCatalogSnapshot` 与 `TestBudgetUsageReconcilerReportsMissingPricingSnapshot`；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 catalog resolver、source/version/model、missing-pricing reconciliation 与 tests；`data-model.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'Test(BudgetUsageReconciler|DirectRunProviderPricingResolvesModelCatalogSnapshot|ExecutePendingDirectRunSuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks)' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：这仍不是真实 provider-billed cost 对账，不证明真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、CLI `CodingProvider` adapter 或真实 provider/live stream 行为。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 CLI generic pause 推进到 CLI CodingProvider typed Desktop handoff service-level：`data-model.md` 新增 `DirectRunCLIHandoff`；Station `executePendingDirectRun` 对 CLI provider 不再写泛化 `unsupported_cli_provider`，而是通过 `directRunCLIProviderHandoffBlocker` 写 `awaiting_desktop_coding_provider` state、`direct_run_desktop_coding_provider_handoff` pending interrupt 与 failed blocking gate；payload 固定 `adapter=desktop_coding_provider`、`handoff_owner=desktop_runtime`、`executor_kind=EXECUTOR_KIND_DESKTOP_DEVICE`、`requested_capabilities=[cli]`、`cli_command_ref=agent_provider.cli_command`，并通过 `blocker.Extra` 同步进 interrupt/gate durable payload；测试断言 provider executor calls=0、raw `traecli exec` 不泄漏。`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 helper、interrupt type、adapter marker、cli command ref、typed blocker metadata 与新 test；`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'TestExecutePendingDirectRunCreatesCLICodingProviderHandoffWithoutProviderCall|TestExecutePendingDirectRunSuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks' -count=1` PASS（cwd=`apps/station/app`）。注意：这仍不是真实 Desktop worker 执行 CLI，不证明真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、真实 provider/live stream 或真实 billing reconciliation。
- P0-09/GAP-UI-01 本轮继续从 catalog-estimated money 推进到 provider-reported billing capture/reconciliation service-level：`ProviderCallResponse` 新增 `BilledMoney/BillingSource/BillingCurrency`；`TaskBudgetUsage` / migration 新增 `estimated_money/provider_billed_money/provider_billing_source/provider_billing_currency`；`createDirectRunBudgetUsageTx` 先保存 catalog estimate，若 provider response 上报 billed evidence，则以 billed money 作为 effective `used_money`，同时保留 estimate 与 billing source 供审计；`BudgetUsageReconciler` mismatch 带 `ProviderBillingSource`，有 provider billing evidence 时不再判为 missing pricing，但仍会报告 catalog expected 与 billed actual mismatch。新增测试覆盖 DirectRun success provider billing persistence、provider billing mismatch、无 pricing 但有 billing evidence 的 missing-pricing 行为；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 provider response fields、budget usage model/migration columns、DirectRun billing capture 与 reconciler tests；`data-model.md`、`ui-implementation-mapping.md`、`feature-matrix.md`、prototype README 与本总账均同步。已验证：`GOWORK=off go test ./subserver/agent/service -run 'Test(BudgetUsageReconciler|ExecutePendingDirectRunSuccessPersistsProviderArtifactGateAndTraceHooks|DirectRunProviderPricingResolvesModelCatalogSnapshot)' -count=1` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS。注意：这仍不证明外部 provider implementations 已填充 billed money，也不证明真实 Host+Station+applet E2E、真实 provider/live stream 或真实 Desktop worker execution。
- GAP-16/P2-12 本轮从最小 read-only evaluator 推进到 Station durable event acceptance evaluator：`atelierAutomatedAcceptancePassed` 改为按 gate id 取 latest non-human gate result，避免已重跑通过的 gate 被历史 failed event 永久污染；`projectAtelierOpenBlockers` 改为维护 gate blocker lifecycle，blocking failed gate 产生 `open` blocker，later passed gate 或 gate-blocked human decision 的 `continue/rerun_failed_node` 推进为 `resolved`，`accept_risk` 推进为 `waived`；`atelierNoOpenBlockers` 按 data-model 的 `Blocker.state in {resolved, waived}` 求值，不再用 `len(openBlockers)==0`；root milestone projection 复用同一 blocker evidence；`normalizeHumanDecisionRouteTx` 会把 pending interrupt payload 的 `gate_id/node_id` 复制到 durable resolved event，保证 projection evaluator 能定位 gate blocker。验证：`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -run 'TestBuildAtelierProjectionSnapshotDerivesProjectCompletion|TestResolveCollaborationInterruptTxRoutesHumanDecisionGateRerun' -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/... -count=1` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍不是 first-class Project/Blocker/ResidualRisk persistence，也不证明完整 AcceptancePredicate registry/runtime 或真实 E2E。
- GAP-01/02/P2-12 本轮从 event-derived projection 继续推进到 Station-owned Blocker/Risk persistence query index：新增 `ProjectBlocker` / `ProjectResidualRisk` GORM models、`010_project_acceptance_indices.sql` migration 与 `AllModels` registration；`TaskEventWriter.appendTx` 在写 durable `agent_task_events` 后事务内同步 `agent_project_blockers` / `agent_project_residual_risks`，blocking gate result、gate-blocked human decision 与 negative feedback 分别维护 blocker/risk lifecycle；`AtelierProjectionService.LoadWorkspace` 新增 `loadAtelierProjectPersistenceByTask`，Project/Milestone/Completion projection 优先消费 persisted blocker/risk records，旧 event-derived path 保留为历史数据 fallback；task purge 会清理新 indexes。新增测试覆盖 writer sync、LoadWorkspace preference、purge cleanup；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 models/migration/writer/loader/test snippets。验证：`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -run 'TestTaskEventWriterPersistsProjectAcceptanceIndexes|TestLoadAtelierWorkspacePrefersPersistedProjectAcceptanceIndexes|TestPurgeAtelierTaskRecordsTxDeletesDurableIndexes' -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/... -count=1` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍不是完整 Project state persistence、AcceptancePredicate registry/runtime、ResidualRisk governance lifecycle 或真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- GAP-03/P2-12 本轮从 Project/Milestone state meta fallback 推进到 Station-owned ProjectState persistence query index：新增 `ProjectState` GORM model、`011_project_state_indices.sql` migration 与 `AllModels` registration；`TaskEventWriter.appendTx` 在写 durable `agent_task_events` 后事务内同步 `agent_project_states`，仅接受 data-model 允许的 Project/Milestone state（含 hyphen→underscore canonicalization），未知 state 不落表；`AtelierProjectionService.LoadWorkspace` 的 `loadAtelierProjectPersistenceByTask` 会读取 ProjectState，Project/Milestone projection 优先消费 persisted state，旧 meta/derived path 保留为 fallback；task purge 会清理 ProjectState index。新增测试覆盖 writer sync、invalid state rejection、LoadWorkspace persisted state 覆盖 task meta fallback、purge cleanup；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 model/migration/writer/loader/test snippets。验证：`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -run 'TestTaskEventWriterPersistsProjectAcceptanceIndexes|TestLoadAtelierWorkspacePrefersPersistedProjectAcceptanceIndexes|TestPurgeAtelierTaskRecordsTxDeletesDurableIndexes' -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/... -count=1` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍不是完整 Project state machine runtime、AcceptancePredicate registry/runtime、真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。

- GAP-16/P2-12 本轮从 AcceptancePredicate verdict snapshot 推进到 Station deterministic predicate executor subset：新增 `AcceptancePredicateEvaluator`，`EvaluateTaskTx` 在 Station transaction scope 内读取 ProjectState/Blocker/ResidualRisk/GateResult/TaskEvent/Task meta 并更新 L0/L1 `last_eval`；支持 `no_open_blockers(project)`、root milestone accepted、residual risks logged/follow_up、`goal_owner_signoff == true`、`memory_candidates.generated == true` 与 `gate-key.passed`，unknown expr fail closed，L2 仍只由 `human_signoff` 控制；`GateRunner.RunAndAppendTx` 在 append durable gate_result 后同 transaction 刷新 predicate verdict。新增测试覆盖 deterministic atoms、unknown fail-closed、L2 skip、GateRunner gate append 刷新 predicate；`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 evaluator/gate runner/frontend non-exposure snippets。验证：`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -run 'TestAcceptancePredicateEvaluatorEvaluatesDeterministicPredicates|TestGateRunnerRunAndAppendTxPersistsGateResult|TestTaskEventWriterPersistsProjectAcceptanceIndexes|TestLoadAtelierWorkspacePrefersPersistedProjectAcceptanceIndexes' -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/service -count=1` PASS；`go test ./apps/station/app/subserver/agent/... -count=1` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这仍不是完整任意表达式 DSL、Project state machine runtime、真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- GAP-03/GAP-16/P2-12 本轮从 ProjectState query index 推进到最小 Station-owned `ProjectStateMachine` runtime：新增 `ProjectStateMachine.AdvanceTaskTx`，在 durable event transaction 中读取 `CollaborationTask`、nodes、GateResult、ProjectBlocker、ProjectResidualRisk、AcceptancePredicate 与 memory candidate evidence，推导 `draft/contracted/executing/blocked/verifying/awaiting_owner_signoff/accepted/escalated` 和 root milestone `planned/active/blocked/accepted/abandoned`，并 upsert `agent_project_states`；`TaskEventWriter.appendTx` 在 artifact/gate/project/predicate/blocker/risk index sync 后刷新 deterministic predicates，再推进 ProjectStateMachine；`AcceptancePredicateEvaluator.rootMilestoneAccepted` 改为直接从 task/node/blocker evidence 推导，避免读取旧 ProjectState stale-cycle；GateRunner append gate_result 通过 writer hook 推进 state。新增测试覆盖无显式 `project_state` payload 的 durable gate event 推进 ProjectStateMachine、writer hook 先刷新 predicate 再推进 state、GateRunner gate append 推进 runtime state；`data-model.md`、prototype README、feature-matrix 与 contract gate 已同步。注意：这是最小 Project/root Milestone runtime，不是完整 TaskGraph/Milestone 独立实体 runtime、完整任意 predicate DSL 或真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- GAP-16/P2-12 本轮从简写 predicate atoms 推进到 data-model deterministic DSL forms：`AcceptancePredicateEvaluator` 新增 `all(b in project.open_blockers : b.state in {resolved, waived})`、`all(t in tasks : t.state == accepted)`、`gate_result(g).passed`、`all(g in gate_plan where g.blocking_level == block : gate_result(g).passed)` 与 `all(p in contract.acceptance where p.level == L2 : p.human_signoff == true)` 等 data-model 形式；gate key matching 继续使用 latest gate result，blocking aggregate 只读取 Station-owned blocking gate result index；unknown expression 仍 fail closed，L2 predicate 本身仍不由 evaluator 改写。`TestAcceptancePredicateEvaluatorEvaluatesDeterministicPredicates` 覆盖 blocker/task/gate/L2 aggregate DSL forms，`atelier:projection-contract-gate` 固定 evaluator/test snippets；prototype README 与 feature matrix 已同步。注意：这仍不是任意表达式 parser、不支持 LLM/主观谓词自动化，也不证明真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- GAP-06/GAP-16/P2-12 本轮从 MilestoneTree/TaskGraph read-only derived projection 推进到最小 Station-owned structure query index：新增 `AtelierMilestone`、`AtelierTaskGraphNode`、`AtelierTaskGraphEdge` GORM models 与 `013_atelier_task_graph_indices.sql` migration，并注册 `AllModels`；`ProjectStateMachine` 在 durable event transaction 末端 materialize root milestone、TaskGraph nodes/edges，node `artifactIds/gateIds` 从 Station-owned `TaskArtifact` / `TaskGateResult` indexes 按 `step_id` 回链；`LoadWorkspace` 优先消费 `agent_atelier_milestones` / `agent_atelier_task_graph_nodes` / `agent_atelier_task_graph_edges`，旧 node/event-derived projection 仅作为 fallback；purge 会清理新 indexes。新增测试覆盖 writer runtime 写 Milestone/TaskGraph index、projection 消费 persisted task graph refs、purge cleanup；`data-model.md`、prototype README、feature-matrix 与 contract gate 已同步。注意：这是最小 projection query index/materializer，不是完整 TaskGraph production/runtime、Milestone 多级实体 runtime、Policy/Defect runtime 或真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。
- GAP-07/P2-12 本轮从 Policy/Defect read-only meta/event projection 推进到最小 Station-owned Policy/Defect query index：新增 `AtelierPolicy`、`AtelierPolicyRule`、`AtelierDefect` GORM models 与 `014_atelier_policy_defect_indices.sql` migration，并注册 `AllModels`；Policy index 使用 task-scoped `policy_projection_id=task_id:policy_id`，避免可复用 policy id 跨 task 覆盖；`ProjectStateMachine` 在 durable event transaction 末端从 task meta materialize Policy/Rule，从 failed gate result materialize proposed Defect；`LoadWorkspace` 优先消费 `agent_atelier_policies` / `agent_atelier_policy_rules` / `agent_atelier_defects`，旧 meta/event-derived projection 仅作为 fallback；purge 会清理新 indexes。新增测试覆盖 writer runtime 写 Policy/Rule/Defect index、projection 消费 persisted policy/defect、purge cleanup；`data-model.md`、prototype README、feature-matrix 与 contract gate 已同步。注意：这是最小 projection query index/materializer，不是完整 Policy engine、Defect governance lifecycle 或真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E。

- P2-03/P2-05 本轮把 official frontend 状态矩阵与 event-stream retry 都推进到 controlled pure-function gate：新增 `application/viewStatus.ts`，将 `loading / empty / ready / reconciling / degraded / disconnected / auth-denied / error` 派生从 `useAtelierController` 抽出为 `deriveAtelierViewStatus`；新增 `application/eventStreamRecovery.ts`，用 `nextAtelierEventStreamRetryDelayMs` 固定 bounded retry policy：有 snapshot 时对 disconnected/generic event-stream subscribe failure 依次 500/1500/5000ms retry，auth-denied / invalid-projection / 无 snapshot / 超过次数不 retry。controller 复用两个 helper，subscribe 失败后保留 snapshot 进入 degraded，成功后恢复 live；同时修正 snapshot commit 直接替换 controller state 的类型/状态丢失问题，改为函数式 merge，避免 provider capabilities、feedback、artifact body 等 controller-local 字段在 load/create/message/decision/task lifecycle 后丢失。`atelier:official-frontend-gate` 现在通过 esbuild 执行状态矩阵和 retry matrix。验证：`pnpm --filter @peers-touch/atelier-official-applet run check` PASS；`pnpm --dir apps/applets/atelier/frontend build` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 controlled local frontend evidence，不证明真实 Desktop Host + Station + applet E2E，也不证明真实网络断线重连或跨重启 cursor recovery E2E。

- P2-03 / official projection artifact refresh invalidates local artifact preview 本轮继续修复 official controller 的 projection event UI 状态 hygiene：当 `subscribeAtelierProjectionEvents` 收到 `artifact.upsert` 且 `applyAtelierProjectionEventWithResult` outcome 为 `applied`，并且该 patch 作用于当前 `selectedTaskId` 时，controller 现在通过 `artifactPreviewInvalidation` 清空 `artifactBody/artifactBodyFetchId/artifactBodyError` 与 `artifactPreviewOpenResponse/artifactPreviewOpenId/artifactPreviewOpenError`。这样 Station projection 更新 selected task 的 artifact metadata/bodyRef/bodyHash/previewTarget 后，official applet 不会继续展示旧 safe body 或旧 Host sandbox renderer session；其它 task 的 artifact projection event 不会误清当前任务预览。`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 新增 `projectionEventHandlerSource` 切片断言，固定 `event.patch.kind === 'artifact.upsert'`、`event.patch.taskId === current.selectedTaskId` 与 preview/body state cleanup；`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 controller 与 gate anchors。验证：`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 projection-codegen-check、projection-contract-gate、bridge-runtime-gate、desktop bridge/typecheck、prototype-gate、official frontend gate/check/build、desktop injection gate，injection integrity expected=actual=`sha256:06cfe41add326f98a56dc1c20ba1ab7e4615d6a26e698391e97155e1aae28699`）；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 official frontend local projection-state hygiene，不改变 Station artifact truth，不证明真实 artifact body fetch、Host sandbox renderer visual runtime、Console Logs runtime stream 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

- P2-03 / official subscription reconnect shows reconciling 本轮修复 official controller 的 task/replay-cursor subscription reconnect UI 状态：`useAtelierController` 的 subscription effect 在调用 `subscribeAtelierProjectionEvents` 前通过纯 helper `stateFromAtelierEventStreamConnecting()` 进入 `eventStreamState: subscribing`、清空 stale stream error，并保持 `loading=false`，因此已有 snapshot surface 在 task-scope reconnect / stream connecting 期间派生为 `reconciling`，不会继续伪装成 live/ready，也不会显示 stale degraded/disconnected 文案。`atelier-official-frontend-gate` 新增 executable transition matrix，证明 connecting transition 清空 stale stream error、派生 `reconciling`；`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 helper、controller usage 与 gate anchor；`docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` 同步 P2-03 evidence 口径。验证：`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 official applet build 与 desktop-injection gate；real-product gate 由 `pnpm run atelier:real-product-gates` 独立覆盖，integrity expected=actual=`sha256:e52c4ffb1e18255e41f083472c0ff067c14264db1fb9c5d9eb103a13ef41b574`）；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 official frontend local reconnect status cleanup，不改变 Station event stream truth，不证明真实 Desktop Host 任意网络故障、真实 product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。

- P2-05 / browser bridge non-snapshot call status race guard 本轮修复 browser prototype `createBridgeAtelierRuntime` 的 non-snapshot Host capability call 状态竞争：`call()` 现在用 `latestCallStatusToken` 固定 latest Host capability call owns runtime status completion，并用 `activeCallRestorableStatus` 记录进入 loading 前的稳定状态；较早 call 的 stale success / stale failure 不能覆盖较晚 call 已经写入的 auth-denied / error / ready 状态；正常单次 non-snapshot success 仍会按 `restoreStatusOnSuccess` 恢复先前状态。`atelier-bridge-runtime-gate` 新增/扩展 `testBridgeNonSnapshotStaleSuccessDoesNotClearNewerError` 与 `testBridgeNonSnapshotStaleFailureDoesNotClearNewerSuccess`，证明 provider capabilities 的较早成功不会清掉 memory confirmation 的较晚 auth-denied，provider capabilities 的较早 failure 也不会清掉 memory confirmation 的较晚 success/ready；`atelier-projection-contract-gate` 固定 runtime token、restorable status、gate tests 与 README anchors；`docs/architecture/atelier/prototype/README.md` 同步 non-snapshot status race evidence。验证：`pnpm run atelier:bridge-runtime-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:projection-contract-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:official-frontend-gate` PASS；`pnpm run atelier:controlled-gates` PASS（包含 official applet build 与 desktop-injection gate；real-product gate 由 `pnpm run atelier:real-product-gates` 独立覆盖，integrity expected=actual=`sha256:5ced1f367ef91d68e92e3176a16c73a8ca97a67200e91aae251e5958650e2f52`）；`git diff --check` PASS。注意：这是 browser prototype local status hygiene，不改变 Host capability response truth、Station provider/memory truth，不证明真实 Desktop Host 并发 capability 调用、真实 product window UI 或完整 Host+Station+applet E2E。
